src/cohost/shim.cljc:922:1 [style/def-fn] - Prefer `defn` instead of `def` wrapping `fn`.
(def RT-nth
  (fn
   ([x i]
    (cond
      (transient-value? x) (memory/t-nth x i)
      (memory/eve-backed? x) (memory/nth-of x i)
      :else (nth x i)))
   ([x i nf]
    (cond
      (transient-value? x) (memory/t-nth x i nf)

     (throw

     (when (nil? (Namespace-find ns))

src/cohost/shim.cljc:1815:1 [style/def-fn] - Prefer `defn` instead of `def` wrapping `fn`.
(def layer-read
  (fn
   ([] (layer-read (core-var-value '*in*)))
   ([opts stream]
    (if (map? opts)
      (let [[f _] (read-one (as-reader stream))]
        (if (= :cohost.shim/eof f)
          (let [e (:eof opts :eofthrow)]
            (if (= :eofthrow e)
              (throw (host/ex* 'java.io.EOFException "EOF while reading"))
              e))
          f))
      (throw
       (host/ex*
        'java.lang.IllegalArgumentException
        "read's two-argument form takes an opts map and a stream"))))
   ([stream] (layer-read stream true nil))
   ([stream eof-error? eof-value] (layer-read stream eof-error? eof-value false))
   ([stream eof-error? eof-value _recursive?]
    (let [[f _] (read-one (as-reader stream))]
      (if (= :cohost.shim/eof f)
        (if eof-error?
          (throw (host/ex* 'java.io.EOFException "EOF while reading"))
          eof-value)
        f)))))
Consider using:

  ([] (layer-read (core-var-value '*in*)))

   (if (map? opts)
     (let [[f _] (read-one (as-reader stream))]
       (if (= :cohost.shim/eof f)
         (let [e (:eof opts :eofthrow)]
           (if (= :eofthrow e)
             (throw (host/ex* 'java.io.EOFException "EOF while reading"))
             e))
         f))
     (throw
      (host/ex*
       'java.lang.IllegalArgumentException
       "read's two-argument form takes an opts map and a stream"))))
  ([stream] (layer-read stream true nil))
  ([stream eof-error? eof-value] (layer-read stream eof-error? eof-value false))
  ([stream eof-error? eof-value _recursive?]
   (let [[f _] (read-one (as-reader stream))]
     (if (= :cohost.shim/eof f)
       (if eof-error?
         (throw (host/ex* 'java.io.EOFException "EOF while reading"))
         eof-value)
       f))))

(def core-Throwable->map
  (fn
   [t]
   (let [chain (loop [e t, seen #{}, out []]
                 (if (or (nil? e) (contains? seen e))
                   out
                   (recur (host/exception-cause e) (conj seen e) (conj out e))))
         base (fn
               [e]
               (let [trace (host/ex-trace e), msg (host/exception-message e)]
                 (cond-> {:type (symbol (str (class-of e)))}
                   (some? msg) (assoc :message msg)
                   (some? (ex-data e)) (assoc :data (ex-data e))
                   (seq trace) (assoc :at (ste->vec (first trace))))))
         root (peek chain)
         phase (get (ex-data t) :clojure.error/phase)]
     (cond-> {:via (mapv base chain), :trace (mapv ste->vec (host/ex-trace root))}
       (some? (host/exception-message root)) (assoc :cause (host/exception-message root))
       (some? (ex-data root)) (assoc :data (ex-data root))
       (some? phase) (assoc :phase phase)))))

      (finally (reset! tx nil))))))

(def new-MultiFn
  (fn
   [name dispatch-fn default hierarchy]
   (let [state (atom
                {:name name,
                 :dispatch-fn dispatch-fn,
                 :default default,
                 :hierarchy hierarchy,
                 :methods {},
                 :prefers {}})

                     (host/ex*
                      'IllegalArgumentException
                      (str

           ([]

src/cohost/shim.cljc:2556:1 [style/def-fn] - Prefer `defn` instead of `def` wrapping `fn`.
(def new-String
  (fn
   ([] "")
   ([x]
    (cond
      (nil? x)
        (throw
         (host/ex*
          'java.lang.NullPointerException
          "Cannot invoke \"String(byte[])\" because \"bytes\" is null"))
      (string? x) x
      (h-sb? x) (h-sb-str x)
      (arr? x)
        (case (array-kind x)
          :chars (apply str (map char-str (seq x)))
          :bytes (decode-bytes x 0 (count x) "UTF-8")
          (str x))
      :else (str x)))
   ([bs cs]
    (cond
      (arr? bs)
        (case (array-kind bs)
          :chars (apply str (map char-str (seq bs)))
          (decode-bytes bs 0 (count bs) (need-charset cs)))
      :else
        (throw
         (host/ex*
          'java.lang.ClassCastException
          (str "class " (class-of bs) " cannot be cast to class [B")))))
   ([a off len] (new-String a off len "UTF-8"))
   ([bs off len cs]
    (case (array-kind bs)
      :chars (apply str (map char-str (take len (drop off (seq bs)))))
      (decode-bytes bs off len (need-charset cs))))))

(def URLEncoder-encode
  (fn
   ([s] (URLEncoder-encode s "UTF-8"))
   ([s enc]
    (let [cs (need-charset enc), out (transient [])]
      (loop [us (seq s)]
        (if (nil? us)
          (apply str (persistent! out))
          (let [u (code-of (first us))]
            (cond
              (url-safe-char? u) (do (conj! out (char-str u)) (recur (next us)))
              (== u 32) (do (conj! out "+") (recur (next us)))
              (<= 55296 u 56319)
                (let [pair (if (second us)
                             (str (char-str u) (char-str (code-of (second us))))
                             (char-str u))]
                  (doseq [b (encode-string-bytes pair cs)]
                    (conj!
                     out
                     "%"
                     (subs hex-digits (bit-shift-right b 4) (inc (bit-shift-right b 4)))
                     (subs hex-digits (bit-and b 15) (inc (bit-and b 15)))))
                  (recur (nnext us)))
              :else
                (do
                 (doseq [b (encode-string-bytes (char-str u) cs)]
                   (conj!
                    out
                    "%"
                    (subs hex-digits (bit-shift-right b 4) (inc (bit-shift-right b 4)))
                    (subs hex-digits (bit-and b 15) (inc (bit-and b 15)))))
                 (recur (next us)))))))))))

src/cohost/shim.cljc:2625:1 [style/def-fn] - Prefer `defn` instead of `def` wrapping `fn`.
(def URLDecoder-decode
  (fn
   ([s] (URLDecoder-decode s "UTF-8"))
   ([s enc]
    (let [cs (need-charset enc), out (transient [])]
      (loop [i 0, bytes []]
        (if (>= i (count s))
          (do
           (when (seq bytes)
             (conj! out (decode-bytes (bytes-of-unsigned bytes) 0 (count bytes) cs)))
           (apply str (persistent! out)))
          (let [ch (nth s i), u (code-of ch)]
            (cond
              (== u 37)
                (let [h1 (when (< (inc i) (count s)) (hex-val (nth s (inc i))))
                      h2 (when (< (+ i 2) (count s)) (hex-val (nth s (+ i 2))))]
                  (if (and h1 h2 (>= h1 0) (>= h2 0))
                    (recur (+ i 3) (conj bytes (+ (bit-shift-left h1 4) h2)))
                    (throw
                     (host/ex*
                      'java.lang.IllegalArgumentException
                      "URLDecoder: Illegal trailing escape (%) pattern"))))
              :else
                (do
                 (when (seq bytes)
                   (conj! out (decode-bytes (bytes-of-unsigned bytes) 0 (count bytes) cs))
                   (loop [] nil))
                 (conj! out (if (== u 43) " " (char-str u)))
                 (recur (inc i) []))))))))))

(def Character-isWhitespace
  (jstatic
   'Character/isWhitespace
   (fn
    [c]
    (let [n (code-of c)]
      (boolean
       (or (<= 9 n 13)
           (<= 28 n 32)
           (== n 5760)
           (<= 8192 n 8198)
           (<= 8200 n 8202)
           (== n 8232)
           (== n 8233)

    (h-sb? s) (new-matcher re (h-sb-str s))

((or (nil? check) (= :ok check)) {:value value})
